<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w0001 -->
# 四级单词 0001 · 调查；探究
中文：n. 询问，打听；调查；探究
单词：###inquiry###
搭配：###conduct an inquiry### 开展调查
例句：Amongst the staff, the ###inquiry### produced different interpretations, but basically everyone acted in accordance with the final decision.
译文：在员工当中，这项调查产生了不同的理解，但基本上每个人都按照最终决定行事。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w0002 -->
# 四级单词 0002 · 解释；理解
中文：n. 解释；口译；理解
单词：###interpretation###
搭配：###different interpretations### 不同的解释
例句：Amongst the staff, the inquiry produced different ###interpretations###, but basically everyone acted in accordance with the final decision.
译文：在员工当中，这项调查产生了不同的理解，但基本上每个人都按照最终决定行事。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w0003 -->
# 四级单词 0003 · 一致；符合
中文：n. 一致，符合
单词：###accordance###
搭配：###in accordance with### 根据；与……一致
例句：Amongst the staff, the inquiry produced different interpretations, but basically everyone acted in ###accordance### with the final decision.
译文：在员工当中，这项调查产生了不同的理解，但基本上每个人都按照最终决定行事。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w0004 -->
# 四级单词 0004 · 基本上
中文：adv. 基本上
单词：###basically###
搭配：###basically the same### 基本相同
例句：Amongst the staff, the inquiry produced different interpretations, but ###basically### everyone acted in accordance with the final decision.
译文：在员工当中，这项调查产生了不同的理解，但基本上每个人都按照最终决定行事。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w0005 -->
# 四级单词 0005 · 在……之中
中文：prep. 在…之中
单词：###amongst###
搭配：###amongst the staff### 在员工当中
例句：###Amongst### the staff, the inquiry produced different interpretations, but basically everyone acted in accordance with the final decision.
译文：在员工当中，这项调查产生了不同的理解，但基本上每个人都按照最终决定行事。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w0008 -->
# 四级单词 0008 · 明确说明
中文：vt. 指定，详细说明；明确说明
单词：###specify###
搭配：###specify the details### 具体说明细节
例句：When you check the report, ###specify### the exact sequence of the specimen tests and their completion dates; other details are secondary.
译文：你核对报告时，要写明标本检测的准确顺序和完成日期；其他细节是次要的。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w0006 -->

搭配：###in the correct sequence### 按正确的顺序

译文：你核对报告时，要写明标本检测的准确顺序和完成日期；其他细节是次要的。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

# 四级单词 0010 · 立场，观点
中文：n. 立场，观点
单词：###standpoint###
搭配：###from a practical standpoint### 从实际的角度看
例句：From a practical ###standpoint###, the undergraduate summarized how tuition changes could be traced to rising costs.
译文：从实际角度来看，这名本科生总结了学费变化如何追溯到成本上升。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

# 四级单词 0015 · 因此，所以
中文：adv. 因此/而，所以
单词：###consequently###
搭配：###and consequently### 因此，所以
例句：There is a considerable contrast between them: one requires continuous practice; conversely, the other is simpler and ###consequently### faster.
译文：它们之间存在着巨大的反差：一种需要持续不断的练习；相反，另一种更简单，因此也更快。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w0016 -->

单词：###industrial###
搭配：###industrial production### 工业生产
例句：To meet its high daily consumption, the ###industrial### plant purchases oil in bulk rather than by the barrel, which saves on expenditure.

译文：旧工厂毛利下降、成本指数持续上升后，总部给它贴上了“低效”的标签。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1645 -->
# 四级单词 1645 · 极小的；显微镜的

例句：Each atom has a ###microscopic### nucleus that fills only a small fraction of its space, so the probability of hitting it is low.
译文：每个原子都有一个极小的原子核，只占它空间的一小部分，所以撞上原子核的概率很低。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

搭配：###increasingly important### 越来越重要
例句：Water is indispensable to life, so scientists are ###increasingly### worried about the impact of pollutants and their implications for health.
译文：水对生命不可或缺，所以科学家越来越担心污染物的影响及其对健康的潜在后果。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1646 -->

单词：###nap###

例句：After vigorous exercise, even a slender runner may shiver with cold and need a ###nap### to recover from muscle strain.
译文：剧烈运动后，即使是身材纤细的跑步者也可能冷得发抖，需要小睡一会儿来缓解肌肉拉伤。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

# 四级单词 0216 · 抢劫；盗窃

单词：###robbery###
搭配：###an armed robbery### 持械抢劫
例句：The shop retained the video of the ###robbery###; it rendered his story useless and proved our suspicion that he was a threat.
译文：商店保留了抢劫录像；它让他的说辞站不住脚，也证实了我们的怀疑——他确实是个威胁。

搭配：###provided that### 只要；假如

例句：The ###attentive### teacher assured us that everyone is capable of learning to cope with and accustom themselves to college life.

搭配：###be bored with### 对……感到厌烦
例句：When the ###boring### speaker cursed at a student, many listeners left in disgust, since no decent teacher would show such contempt.

搭配：###show contempt for### 对……表示轻蔑
例句：When the boring speaker cursed at a student, many listeners left in disgust, since no decent teacher would show such ###contempt###.
译文：那个乏味的演讲者冲一名学生咒骂时，许多听众厌恶地离开了，因为正派的老师不会表现出这样的蔑视。

搭配：###curse under one's breath### 小声咒骂

搭配：###to one's disgust### 令某人厌恶的是

搭配：###deceive sb. into doing sth.### 骗某人做某事
例句：I value honesty and dislike people who ###deceive### others; a plan that is dependent on falsehood will fail sooner or later.
译文：我看重诚实，不喜欢骗人的人；靠谎言撑着的计划迟早会失败。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w0248 -->

搭配：###be dependent on### 依赖
例句：I value honesty and dislike people who deceive others; a plan that is ###dependent### on falsehood will fail sooner or later.

<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1659 -->

搭配：###truth and falsehood### 真与假
例句：I value honesty and dislike people who deceive others; a plan that is dependent on ###falsehood### will fail sooner or later.

<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w0251 -->
# 四级单词 0251 · 伪装；掩饰

搭配：###in disguise### 伪装的；乔装的
例句：She tried to ###disguise### her distress before the exam, but I could see hesitation in her eyes; still, she did not despair.
译文：考试前她想掩饰内心的苦恼，可我能从她眼里看出犹豫；不过，她没有绝望。

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w0252 -->

搭配：###in distress### 处于困境；苦恼
例句：She tried to disguise her ###distress### before the exam, but I could see hesitation in her eyes; still, she did not despair.
译文：考试前她想掩饰内心的苦恼，可我能从她眼里看出犹豫；不过，她没有绝望。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1660 -->

搭配：###without hesitation### 毫不犹豫
例句：She tried to disguise her distress before the exam, but I could see ###hesitation### in her eyes; still, she did not despair.
译文：考试前她想掩饰内心的苦恼，可我能从她眼里看出犹豫；不过，她没有绝望。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w0250 -->

搭配：###fall into despair### 陷入绝望
例句：She tried to disguise her distress before the exam, but I could see hesitation in her eyes; still, she did not ###despair###.

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w0254 -->

搭配：###an earnest effort### 认真的努力

<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w0257 -->

单词：###evil###
搭配：###fight against evil### 与邪恶作斗争
例句：In the old story, the earnest young hero endures great pain, but his enthusiasm never fades until good defeats ###evil###.

搭配：###exaggerate the truth### 夸大事实

搭配：###scarcely any### 几乎没有

中文：n. 效用，有用，实用；杂物间；公用设施
单词：###utility###
搭配：###a utility room### 杂物间
例句：The scent of old food filled the ###utility### room, so I threw the spoiled stuff away; the air there was stuffy too.
译文：杂物间里满是剩饭的气味，我就把变质的东西扔了；那里的空气也很闷。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

# 四级单词 0528 · 迷人的

单词：###charming###
搭配：###a charming smile### 迷人的微笑
例句：What a ###charming### cottage! — Thanks. We planted roses by the hedge and lilies along the path; it looks quite homely.
译文：多迷人的小屋！——谢谢。我们在树篱边种了玫瑰，沿着小路种了百合，看着挺温馨的。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w0706 -->
# 四级单词 0706 · 宿舍
中文：n. 宿舍
单词：###dorm###
搭配：###live in a dorm### 住宿舍
例句：Sorry to bother you, but could I borrow your hammer? The shelf in my ###dorm### is awfully loose — a screw fell out.
译文：抱歉打扰，能借一下你的锤子吗？我宿舍的架子松得厉害——掉了一颗螺丝。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w0707 -->
# 四级单词 0707 · 非常
中文：adv. 非常，很
单词：###awfully###
搭配：###awfully sorry### 非常抱歉
例句：Sorry to bother you, but could I borrow your hammer? The shelf in my dorm is ###awfully### loose — a screw fell out.
译文：抱歉打扰，能借一下你的锤子吗？我宿舍的架子松得厉害——掉了一颗螺丝。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w0708 -->
# 四级单词 0708 · 螺丝
中文：n. 螺旋，螺丝 vt. 拧紧
单词：###screw###
搭配：###tighten a screw### 拧紧螺丝
例句：Sorry to bother you, but could I borrow your hammer? The shelf in my dorm is awfully loose — a ###screw### fell out.
译文：抱歉打扰，能借一下你的锤子吗？我宿舍的架子松得厉害——掉了一颗螺丝。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w0709 -->
# 四级单词 0709 · 醋
中文：n. 醋
单词：###vinegar###
搭配：###a bottle of vinegar### 一瓶醋
例句：What a mess! Who spilled ###vinegar### on my new mat? It'll leave a stain! — My apologies, I'll clean it right away.
译文：真是一团糟！谁把醋洒在我的新垫子上了？会留下污渍的！——对不起，我马上擦干净。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w0710 -->
# 四级单词 0710 · 垫子
中文：n. 席子；草席；垫子
单词：###mat###
搭配：###a yoga mat### 瑜伽垫
例句：What a mess! Who spilled vinegar on my new ###mat###? It'll leave a stain! — My apologies, I'll clean it right away.
译文：真是一团糟！谁把醋洒在我的新垫子上了？会留下污渍的！——对不起，我马上擦干净。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w0711 -->
# 四级单词 0711 · 污渍；弄脏
中文：vt. 沾污；给…着色；弄脏 n. 污渍
单词：###stain###
搭配：###a coffee stain### 咖啡渍
例句：What a mess! Who spilled vinegar on my new mat? It'll leave a ###stain###! — My apologies, I'll clean it right away.
译文：真是一团糟！谁把醋洒在我的新垫子上了？会留下污渍的！——对不起，我马上擦干净。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w0712 -->

中文：n. 道歉，认错，谢罪
单词：###apology###
搭配：###make an apology### 道歉
例句：What a mess! Who spilled vinegar on my new mat? It'll leave a stain! — My ###apologies###, I'll clean it right away.
译文：真是一团糟！谁把醋洒在我的新垫子上了？会留下污渍的！——对不起，我马上擦干净。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w0713 -->
# 四级单词 0713 · 阳光
中文：n. (直射)日光，阳光
单词：###sunshine###
搭配：###in the sunshine### 在阳光下
例句：Phew, the ###sunshine### is so strong! — Let's sit under that pine; it's shady and there's a lovely breeze.
译文：哎呀，太阳真毒！——我们去那棵松树下坐吧，那里有阴凉，还有凉爽的微风。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w0714 -->

单词：###pine###
搭配：###a pine forest### 松树林
例句：Phew, the sunshine is so strong! — Let's sit under that ###pine###; it's shady and there's a lovely breeze.
译文：哎呀，太阳真毒！——我们去那棵松树下坐吧，那里有阴凉，还有凉爽的微风。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w0715 -->

中文：adj. 成荫的，阴凉的
单词：###shady###
搭配：###a shady garden### 阴凉的花园
例句：Phew, the sunshine is so strong! — Let's sit under that pine; it's ###shady### and there's a lovely breeze.
译文：哎呀，太阳真毒！——我们去那棵松树下坐吧，那里有阴凉，还有凉爽的微风。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w0716 -->

中文：n. 微风，和风
单词：###breeze###
搭配：###a cool breeze### 凉爽的微风
例句：Phew, the sunshine is so strong! — Let's sit under that pine; it's shady and there's a lovely ###breeze###.
译文：哎呀，太阳真毒！——我们去那棵松树下坐吧，那里有阴凉，还有凉爽的微风。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w0717 -->
# 四级单词 0717 · 同事
中文：n. 同事，同僚
单词：###colleague###
搭配：###a former colleague### 前同事
例句：Are you free on Saturday? My ###colleague### has tickets for a famous composer's concert in the gymnasium. It'll be really enjoyable!
译文：周六有空吗？我同事有几张票，是一位著名作曲家在体育馆的音乐会。一定很好玩！
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1708 -->

单词：###composer###
搭配：###a classical composer### 古典作曲家
例句：Are you free on Saturday? My colleague has tickets for a famous ###composer###'s concert in the gymnasium. It'll be really enjoyable!
译文：周六有空吗？我同事有几张票，是一位著名作曲家在体育馆的音乐会。一定很好玩！
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w0719 -->

搭配：###the school gymnasium### 学校体育馆
例句：Are you free on Saturday? My colleague has tickets for a famous composer's concert in the ###gymnasium###. It'll be really enjoyable!
译文：周六有空吗？我同事有几张票，是一位著名作曲家在体育馆的音乐会。一定很好玩！
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1709 -->

中文：adj. 有趣的，愉快的；令人愉快的
单词：###enjoyable###
搭配：###an enjoyable evening### 愉快的夜晚
例句：Are you free on Saturday? My colleague has tickets for a famous composer's concert in the gymnasium. It'll be really ###enjoyable###!
译文：周六有空吗？我同事有几张票，是一位著名作曲家在体育馆的音乐会。一定很好玩！
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w0721 -->
# 四级单词 0721 · （火车）车厢
中文：n. 客车厢；四轮马车
单词：###carriage###
搭配：###a first-class carriage### 头等车厢
例句：Excuse me, is this ###carriage### going to the seaport? — No, it heads northeast. Ask the attendant at the next stop.
译文：请问这节车厢去海港吗？——不去，它往东北走。到下一站问问乘务员吧。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w0722 -->

搭配：###a busy seaport### 繁忙的海港
例句：Excuse me, is this carriage going to the ###seaport###? — No, it heads northeast. Ask the attendant at the next stop.
译文：请问这节车厢去海港吗？——不去，它往东北走。到下一站问问乘务员吧。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w0723 -->

单词：###northeast###
搭配：###in the northeast### 在东北部
例句：Excuse me, is this carriage going to the seaport? — No, it heads ###northeast###. Ask the attendant at the next stop.
译文：请问这节车厢去海港吗？——不去，它往东北走。到下一站问问乘务员吧。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1710 -->

搭配：###a flight attendant### 空乘
例句：Excuse me, is this carriage going to the seaport? — No, it heads northeast. Ask the ###attendant### at the next stop.
译文：请问这节车厢去海港吗？——不去，它往东北走。到下一站问问乘务员吧。

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w0725 -->
# 四级单词 0725 · 脸颊
中文：n. 面颊，脸蛋；脸颊
单词：###cheek###
搭配：###rosy cheeks### 红润的脸颊
例句：My ###cheek### and chin are badly swollen. — You must have a bad tooth; see the dentist right away.
译文：我的脸颊和下巴肿得厉害。——你肯定是牙出了问题，马上去看牙医吧。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w0726 -->

单词：###chin###
搭配：###a double chin### 双下巴
例句：My cheek and ###chin### are badly swollen. — You must have a bad tooth; see the dentist right away.
译文：我的脸颊和下巴肿得厉害。——你肯定是牙出了问题，马上去看牙医吧。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w0727 -->

中文：adv. 坏，差；严重地
单词：###badly###
搭配：###be badly hurt### 伤得很重
例句：My cheek and chin are ###badly### swollen. — You must have a bad tooth; see the dentist right away.
译文：我的脸颊和下巴肿得厉害。——你肯定是牙出了问题，马上去看牙医吧。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w0728 -->

单词：###swell###
搭配：###a swollen ankle### 肿起的脚踝
例句：My cheek and chin are badly ###swollen###. — You must have a bad tooth; see the dentist right away.
译文：我的脸颊和下巴肿得厉害。——你肯定是牙出了问题，马上去看牙医吧。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w0729 -->
# 四级单词 0729 · 枕头
中文：n. 枕头
单词：###pillow###
搭配：###a soft pillow### 软枕头
例句：The baby's asleep on my ###pillow### — please speak softly, don't bang the door, and turn off the television and the printer.
译文：宝宝在我的枕头上睡着了——请小声说话，别砰地关门，把电视和打印机都关掉。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w0730 -->

单词：###softly###
搭配：###speak softly### 轻声说话
例句：The baby's asleep on my pillow — please speak ###softly###, don't bang the door, and turn off the television and the printer.
译文：宝宝在我的枕头上睡着了——请小声说话，别砰地关门，把电视和打印机都关掉。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w0732 -->

中文：n. 电视；电视机
单词：###television###
搭配：###watch television### 看电视
例句：The baby's asleep on my pillow — please speak softly, don't bang the door, and turn off the ###television### and the printer.
译文：宝宝在我的枕头上睡着了——请小声说话，别砰地关门，把电视和打印机都关掉。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1711 -->

中文：n. 印刷工；印花工；打印机
单词：###printer###
搭配：###a laser printer### 激光打印机
例句：The baby's asleep on my pillow — please speak softly, don't bang the door, and turn off the television and the ###printer###.
译文：宝宝在我的枕头上睡着了——请小声说话，别砰地关门，把电视和打印机都关掉。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w0733 -->
# 四级单词 0733 · 年轻的
中文：adj. 年轻的，青年的
单词：###youthful###
搭配：###youthful energy### 青春活力
例句：You look so ###youthful### and cheerful today, darling! — Thanks, it's all the laughter at home.
译文：亲爱的，你今天看上去真年轻、真开心！——谢谢，都是因为家里笑声多。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w0734 -->

中文：adj. 快乐的，愉快的
单词：###cheerful###
搭配：###a cheerful smile### 愉快的笑容
例句：You look so youthful and ###cheerful### today, darling! — Thanks, it's all the laughter at home.
译文：亲爱的，你今天看上去真年轻、真开心！——谢谢，都是因为家里笑声多。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w0735 -->

中文：n. 亲爱的人；宠儿
单词：###darling###
搭配：###my darling### 我亲爱的
例句：You look so youthful and cheerful today, ###darling###! — Thanks, it's all the laughter at home.
译文：亲爱的，你今天看上去真年轻、真开心！——谢谢，都是因为家里笑声多。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w0736 -->
# 四级单词 0736 · 笑声
中文：n. 笑，笑声
单词：###laughter###
搭配：###burst into laughter### 突然大笑
例句：You look so youthful and cheerful today, darling! — Thanks, it's all the ###laughter### at home.
译文：亲爱的，你今天看上去真年轻、真开心！——谢谢，都是因为家里笑声多。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w0737 -->
# 四级单词 0737 · 扫帚
中文：n. 扫帚
单词：###broom###
搭配：###sweep with a broom### 用扫帚扫
例句：I can't find the ###broom### anywhere! — Look in the room upstairs, behind the couch and the ladder.
译文：我哪儿都找不到扫帚！——去楼上的房间找找，在沙发和梯子后面。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w0738 -->

单词：###upstairs###
搭配：###go upstairs### 上楼
例句：I can't find the broom anywhere! — Look in the room ###upstairs###, behind the couch and the ladder.
译文：我哪儿都找不到扫帚！——去楼上的房间找找，在沙发和梯子后面。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1712 -->
# 四级单词 1712 · 长沙发

单词：###couch###
搭配：###sit on the couch### 坐在沙发上
例句：I can't find the broom anywhere! — Look in the room upstairs, behind the ###couch### and the ladder.
译文：我哪儿都找不到扫帚！——去楼上的房间找找，在沙发和梯子后面。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w0740 -->

中文：n. 梯子，梯状物
单词：###ladder###
搭配：###climb a ladder### 爬梯子
例句：I can't find the broom anywhere! — Look in the room upstairs, behind the couch and the ###ladder###.
译文：我哪儿都找不到扫帚！——去楼上的房间找找，在沙发和梯子后面。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w0741 -->
# 四级单词 0741 · 服务员
中文：n. 侍者，服务员
单词：###waiter###
搭配：###call the waiter### 叫服务员
例句：Shall I ask the ###waiter### for some refreshments? — Just a mug of tea for me; we'll have supper later.
译文：我叫服务员上点茶点吧？——给我一杯茶就行，我们待会儿还要吃晚饭。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w0742 -->

搭配：###light refreshments### 茶点
例句：Shall I ask the waiter for some ###refreshments###? — Just a mug of tea for me; we'll have supper later.
译文：我叫服务员上点茶点吧？——给我一杯茶就行，我们待会儿还要吃晚饭。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w0743 -->

单词：###mug###
搭配：###a mug of coffee### 一大杯咖啡
例句：Shall I ask the waiter for some refreshments? — Just a ###mug### of tea for me; we'll have supper later.
译文：我叫服务员上点茶点吧？——给我一杯茶就行，我们待会儿还要吃晚饭。

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w0744 -->

中文：n. 晚饭，晚餐
单词：###supper###
搭配：###have supper### 吃晚饭
例句：Shall I ask the waiter for some refreshments? — Just a mug of tea for me; we'll have ###supper### later.
译文：我叫服务员上点茶点吧？——给我一杯茶就行，我们待会儿还要吃晚饭。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w0745 -->
# 四级单词 0745 · 公牛
中文：n. 公牛；雄的象
单词：###bull###
搭配：###a fierce bull### 凶猛的公牛
例句：Careful! That ###bull### looks aggressive. — Relax, there's a strong fence, and the keeper is holding a whip.
译文：小心！那头公牛看起来很凶。——别担心，有结实的围栏，饲养员手里还拿着鞭子。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w0746 -->

搭配：###aggressive behaviour### 攻击性行为
例句：Careful! That bull looks ###aggressive###. — Relax, there's a strong fence, and the keeper is holding a whip.
译文：小心！那头公牛看起来很凶。——别担心，有结实的围栏，饲养员手里还拿着鞭子。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w0747 -->

搭配：###a zoo keeper### 动物园饲养员
例句：Careful! That bull looks aggressive. — Relax, there's a strong fence, and the ###keeper### is holding a whip.
译文：小心！那头公牛看起来很凶。——别担心，有结实的围栏，饲养员手里还拿着鞭子。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w0748 -->

中文：vt. 鞭笞；搅打；鞭打 n. 鞭子
单词：###whip###
搭配：###crack a whip### 甩鞭子
例句：Careful! That bull looks aggressive. — Relax, there's a strong fence, and the keeper is holding a ###whip###.
译文：小心！那头公牛看起来很凶。——别担心，有结实的围栏，饲养员手里还拿着鞭子。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w0749 -->

<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w0781 -->

例句：Travelling abroad, particularly in remote regions, widens our outlook and ###deepens### our understanding of cultural distinctions.

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w0789 -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w0793 -->
# 四级单词 0793 · 技术的

搭配：###technical support### 技术支持
例句：The investigation produced conclusive evidence that the crash was primarily caused by human error, not ###technical### failure.
译文：调查得出了确凿的证据：这次撞车主要是人为失误造成的，而不是技术故障。

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w0794 -->
# 四级单词 0794 · 无疑地
中文：adv. 毋庸置疑地，肯定地；无疑地
单词：###undoubtedly###
搭配：###undoubtedly true### 无疑是真的
例句：Failure is ###undoubtedly### painful, yet it often offers lessons more valuable than success, so we should not feel hopeless or miserable.
译文：失败无疑是痛苦的，但它常常带来比成功更宝贵的教训，所以我们不应感到绝望或痛苦。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w0884 -->

例句：Frost arrived early this year, killing the tender ###buds### and blossoms and leaving the fields bare and the farmers in gloom.

译文：你看比赛了吗？我们的运动员打破了世界纪录！——太棒了！多么欢欣的胜利时刻！
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w0916 -->

<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w0917 -->

搭配：###turn ... upside-down### 把……翻过来
例句：Look at you — soaked! — The wind turned my umbrella ###upside-down### and ruined my stylish coat. — Go and bathe, and take my raincoat next time.
译文：看你，浑身湿透了！——风把我的伞吹翻了，还毁了我那件时髦的外套。——快去洗个澡，下次带上我的雨衣。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1737 -->

例句：Look at you — soaked! — The wind turned my umbrella upside-down and ruined my ###stylish### coat. — Go and bathe, and take my raincoat next time.
译文：看你，浑身湿透了！——风把我的伞吹翻了，还毁了我那件时髦的外套。——快去洗个澡，下次带上我的雨衣。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

译文：看你，浑身湿透了！——风把我的伞吹翻了，还毁了我那件时髦的外套。——快去洗个澡，下次带上我的雨衣。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

# 四级单词 0925 · 数学
中文：n. 数学
单词：###mathematics###
搭配：###applied mathematics### 应用数学
例句：Help! This ###mathematics### homework is driving me insane! — Which part? — The algebra. — Just multiply both sides by three.
译文：救命！这数学作业要把我逼疯了！——哪部分？——代数。——两边同时乘以三就行。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

译文：关于用动物做实验的争论仍在继续：有人说它残忍，也有人认为它是一种令人不快的必要。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w0968 -->
# 四级单词 0968 · 必要；必需品
中文：n. 必要性；必然性；必需品
单词：###necessity###
搭配：###the necessities of life### 生活必需品
例句：The argument over experimental use of animals goes on: some call it cruel, while others see it as an unpleasant ###necessity###.
译文：关于用动物做实验的争论仍在继续：有人说它残忍，也有人认为它是一种令人不快的必要。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w0969 -->
# 四级单词 0969 · 有希望的；抱希望的
中文：adj. 有希望的；抱希望的
单词：###hopeful###
搭配：###be hopeful about ...### 对……抱有希望
例句：A ###hopeful### attitude helps us recover readily from emotional pain and find happiness in small things.
译文：充满希望的心态能帮助我们从情感创伤中迅速恢复，并在小事中找到幸福。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w0970 -->

译文：充满希望的心态能帮助我们从情感创伤中迅速恢复，并在小事中找到幸福。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w0971 -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w0973 -->
# 四级单词 0973 · 不像
中文：adj. 不同的 prep. 不像，与…不同
单词：###unlike###
搭配：###unlike others### 和别人不同
例句：###Unlike### their parents, many young people are willing to change jobs and do not regard a stable career as their utmost goal.
译文：与父母不同，许多年轻人愿意换工作，并不把稳定的职业视为最高目标。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w0974 -->

搭配：###be willing to do### 愿意做
例句：Unlike their parents, many young people are ###willing### to change jobs and do not regard a stable career as their utmost goal.
译文：与父母不同，许多年轻人愿意换工作，并不把稳定的职业视为最高目标。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w0975 -->

搭配：###a stable job### 稳定的工作
例句：Unlike their parents, many young people are willing to change jobs and do not regard a ###stable### career as their utmost goal.

<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w0977 -->

中文：n. 破坏，毁灭，消灭

搭配：###cause destruction### 造成破坏
例句：The ###destruction### caused by earthquakes and floods reminds us how powerful nature is and how relatively weak humans are, despite all our capabilities.
译文：地震和洪水造成的破坏提醒我们：大自然多么强大，而人类尽管本领不小，却相对弱小。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w0978 -->

例句：The destruction caused by earthquakes and floods reminds us how powerful nature is and how relatively weak humans are, despite all our ###capabilities###.

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w0981 -->

例句：In a democracy, every citizen enjoys ###equality### before the law and the privilege of electing representatives.

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w0983 -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w0985 -->
# 四级单词 0985 · 烟囱

单词：###chimney###
搭配：###a factory chimney### 工厂烟囱
例句：Smoke from factory ###chimneys### carries harmful substances in gaseous form, which later fall as acid rain and seriously damage crop cultivation.
译文：工厂烟囱冒出的烟带着气态的有害物质，这些物质后来变成酸雨落下，严重破坏庄稼的种植。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

例句：Smoke from factory chimneys carries harmful substances in ###gaseous### form, which later fall as acid rain and seriously damage crop cultivation.

<!-- /leiji-note -->

搭配：###the cultivation of rice### 水稻种植

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w0993 -->

搭配：###drink through a straw### 用吸管喝

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1002 -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1005 -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1008 -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1010 -->

搭配：###a research laboratory### 研究实验室

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1094 -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1763 -->
# 四级单词 1763 · 不理智的，荒谬的
中文：adj. 不合理的，不理性的；不理智的；荒谬的
单词：###irrational###
搭配：###an irrational fear### 莫名的恐惧
例句：Did you hear scratching above the ceiling? I hope it's not a rat — rats terrify me! — Relax, it's the wind; you're being ###irrational###.
译文：你听见天花板上的抓挠声了吗？但愿不是老鼠——我最怕老鼠了！——放松，是风；你太不理智了。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1098 -->
# 四级单词 1098 · 消遣
中文：n. 消遣，娱乐
单词：###pastime###
搭配：###a popular pastime### 流行的消遣
例句：What do you do in your spare time? — My favourite ###pastime### is to sketch landscapes or photograph swallows near the pond.
译文：你空闲时做什么？——我最喜欢的消遣是画风景速写，或者在池塘边拍燕子。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1099 -->
# 四级单词 1099 · 速写；画草图
中文：n. 略图；速写；概略 v. 画草图
单词：###sketch###
搭配：###make a sketch### 画素描
例句：What do you do in your spare time? — My favourite pastime is to ###sketch### landscapes or photograph swallows near the pond.
译文：你空闲时做什么？——我最喜欢的消遣是画风景速写，或者在池塘边拍燕子。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1100 -->
# 四级单词 1100 · 拍照；照片
中文：n. 照/相片 v. 拍照
单词：###photograph###
搭配：###photograph the scenery### 拍风景
例句：What do you do in your spare time? — My favourite pastime is to sketch landscapes or ###photograph### swallows near the pond.
译文：你空闲时做什么？——我最喜欢的消遣是画风景速写，或者在池塘边拍燕子。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1101 -->
# 四级单词 1101 · 燕子
中文：n. 燕子
单词：###swallow###
搭配：###One swallow does not make a summer.### 一燕不成夏。
例句：What do you do in your spare time? — My favourite pastime is to sketch landscapes or photograph ###swallows### near the pond.
译文：你空闲时做什么？——我最喜欢的消遣是画风景速写，或者在池塘边拍燕子。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1764 -->
# 四级单词 1764 · 纸币，钞票
中文：n. 纸币，钞票
单词：###banknote###
搭配：###a ten-dollar banknote### 十美元纸币
例句：I've lost my purse with all my ###banknotes###! — Check your pocket? — Empty! It's surely at the ticket booth. — Let's dash back!
译文：我的钱包丢了，钞票全在里面！——口袋里看过吗？——空的！肯定落在售票亭了。——快跑回去！
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1103 -->
# 四级单词 1103 · 肯定
中文：adv. 确实；稳当地；肯定
单词：###surely###
搭配：###slowly but surely### 慢慢地但稳步地
例句：I've lost my purse with all my banknotes! — Check your pocket? — Empty! It's ###surely### at the ticket booth. — Let's dash back!
译文：我的钱包丢了，钞票全在里面！——口袋里看过吗？——空的！肯定落在售票亭了。——快跑回去！
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1104 -->
# 四级单词 1104 · 亭；货摊
中文：n. 货摊；公用电话亭
单词：###booth###
搭配：###a ticket booth### 售票亭
例句：I've lost my purse with all my banknotes! — Check your pocket? — Empty! It's surely at the ticket ###booth###. — Let's dash back!
译文：我的钱包丢了，钞票全在里面！——口袋里看过吗？——空的！肯定落在售票亭了。——快跑回去！
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1105 -->
# 四级单词 1105 · 猛冲
中文：vt. 使猛撞；溅 n. 猛冲
单词：###dash###
搭配：###make a dash for ...### 冲向……
例句：I've lost my purse with all my banknotes! — Check your pocket? — Empty! It's surely at the ticket booth. — Let's ###dash### back!
译文：我的钱包丢了，钞票全在里面！——口袋里看过吗？——空的！肯定落在售票亭了。——快跑回去！
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1106 -->
# 四级单词 1106 · 霉；模子
中文：n. 模子，模型 vt. 浇铸；霉
单词：###mould###
搭配：###grow mould### 发霉
例句：Oh dear! There's ###mould### on the bread and the apples have gone bad. — Throw them out; their odour is unbearable and could sicken us.
译文：哎呀！面包长霉了，苹果也坏了。——扔掉吧，那味道受不了，还可能让我们生病。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1108 -->
# 四级单词 1108 · 气味
中文：n. 气味，香气；味道
单词：###odour###
搭配：###a strong odour### 浓烈的气味
例句：Oh dear! There's mould on the bread and the apples have gone bad. — Throw them out; their ###odour### is unbearable and could sicken us.
译文：哎呀！面包长霉了，苹果也坏了。——扔掉吧，那味道受不了，还可能让我们生病。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1109 -->
# 四级单词 1109 · 难以忍受的
中文：adj. 难堪的，忍受不了的；难以忍受的
单词：###unbearable###
搭配：###unbearable pain### 难以忍受的疼痛
例句：Oh dear! There's mould on the bread and the apples have gone bad. — Throw them out; their odour is ###unbearable### and could sicken us.
译文：哎呀！面包长霉了，苹果也坏了。——扔掉吧，那味道受不了，还可能让我们生病。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1765 -->
# 四级单词 1765 · 使生病；使厌恶
中文：v. 使生病；使厌恶
单词：###sicken###
搭配：###be sickened by### 对……感到恶心
例句：Oh dear! There's mould on the bread and the apples have gone bad. — Throw them out; their odour is unbearable and could ###sicken### us.
译文：哎呀！面包长霉了，苹果也坏了。——扔掉吧，那味道受不了，还可能让我们生病。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1111 -->
# 四级单词 1111 · 唤醒
中文：vi. 醒来；唤醒 vt. 弄醒
单词：###waken###
搭配：###waken sb. up### 叫醒某人
例句：Why do you look so sleepy? — Noise from upstairs ###wakened### me at three — so tiresome — and my new bed is stiff and uncomfortable.
译文：你怎么这么困？——楼上的噪音三点就把我吵醒了，真烦人；而且我的新床又硬又不舒服。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1766 -->
# 四级单词 1766 · 令人厌烦的
中文：adj. 使人厌倦的，讨厌的；令人厌烦的
单词：###tiresome###
搭配：###a tiresome job### 烦人的工作
例句：Why do you look so sleepy? — Noise from upstairs wakened me at three — so ###tiresome### — and my new bed is stiff and uncomfortable.
译文：你怎么这么困？——楼上的噪音三点就把我吵醒了，真烦人；而且我的新床又硬又不舒服。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1112 -->
# 四级单词 1112 · 硬的；僵硬的
中文：adj. 硬的；僵直的；僵硬的
单词：###stiff###
搭配：###a stiff neck### 脖子发僵
例句：Why do you look so sleepy? — Noise from upstairs wakened me at three — so tiresome — and my new bed is ###stiff### and uncomfortable.
译文：你怎么这么困？——楼上的噪音三点就把我吵醒了，真烦人；而且我的新床又硬又不舒服。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1113 -->
# 四级单词 1113 · 不舒服的
中文：adj. 不舒服的；不自在的
单词：###uncomfortable###
搭配：###feel uncomfortable### 感到不自在
例句：Why do you look so sleepy? — Noise from upstairs wakened me at three — so tiresome — and my new bed is stiff and ###uncomfortable###.
译文：你怎么这么困？——楼上的噪音三点就把我吵醒了，真烦人；而且我的新床又硬又不舒服。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1114 -->
# 四级单词 1114 · 完全地
中文：adv. 很，完全
单词：###perfectly###
搭配：###perfectly normal### 完全正常
例句：How was the beach holiday? — ###Perfectly### relaxing! I did yoga on the sandy beach every morning, and the hotel's hospitality was great.
译文：海边度假怎么样？——太放松了！我每天早上在沙滩上做瑜伽，酒店的服务也特别周到。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1767 -->
# 四级单词 1767 · 瑜伽
中文：n. 瑜伽
单词：###yoga###
搭配：###do yoga### 做瑜伽
例句：How was the beach holiday? — Perfectly relaxing! I did ###yoga### on the sandy beach every morning, and the hotel's hospitality was great.
译文：海边度假怎么样？——太放松了！我每天早上在沙滩上做瑜伽，酒店的服务也特别周到。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1116 -->
# 四级单词 1116 · 沙的
中文：adj. 沙的，多沙的
单词：###sandy###
搭配：###a sandy beach### 沙滩
例句：How was the beach holiday? — Perfectly relaxing! I did yoga on the ###sandy### beach every morning, and the hotel's hospitality was great.
译文：海边度假怎么样？——太放松了！我每天早上在沙滩上做瑜伽，酒店的服务也特别周到。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1768 -->
# 四级单词 1768 · 好客；殷勤招待
中文：n. 好客，殷勤的款待
单词：###hospitality###
搭配：###thank sb. for their hospitality### 感谢某人的款待
例句：How was the beach holiday? — Perfectly relaxing! I did yoga on the sandy beach every morning, and the hotel's ###hospitality### was great.
译文：海边度假怎么样？——太放松了！我每天早上在沙滩上做瑜伽，酒店的服务也特别周到。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1118 -->
# 四级单词 1118 · 粗暴地；大致
中文：adv. 粗糙地，粗略地；粗暴地；大致
单词：###roughly###
搭配：###roughly speaking### 粗略地说
例句：Why is your wrist wrapped? — Someone pushed me ###roughly### at athletic training, and I twisted it. — Does it hurt? — Just a dull pain now.
译文：你手腕怎么包着？——田径训练时有人猛推了我一下，我扭伤了。——疼吗？——现在只是隐隐地疼。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1769 -->
# 四级单词 1769 · 运动的；体格健壮的
中文：adj. 强壮的；擅长运动的；运动的，运动员的；体格健壮的
单词：###athletic###
搭配：###athletic training### 田径训练
例句：Why is your wrist wrapped? — Someone pushed me roughly at ###athletic### training, and I twisted it. — Does it hurt? — Just a dull pain now.
译文：你手腕怎么包着？——田径训练时有人猛推了我一下，我扭伤了。——疼吗？——现在只是隐隐地疼。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1120 -->
# 四级单词 1120 · 扭伤；拧
中文：vt. 捻；拧；扭伤 vi. 扭弯 n. 扭弯
单词：###twist###
搭配：###twist one's ankle### 扭伤脚踝
例句：Why is your wrist wrapped? — Someone pushed me roughly at athletic training, and I ###twisted### it. — Does it hurt? — Just a dull pain now.
译文：你手腕怎么包着？——田径训练时有人猛推了我一下，我扭伤了。——疼吗？——现在只是隐隐地疼。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1121 -->
# 四级单词 1121 · 乏味的；隐约的；暗淡的
中文：adj. 枯燥的；不鲜明的；乏味的；（疼痛）隐约的；暗淡的
单词：###dull###
搭配：###a dull ache### 隐痛
例句：Why is your wrist wrapped? — Someone pushed me roughly at athletic training, and I twisted it. — Does it hurt? — Just a ###dull### pain now.
译文：你手腕怎么包着？——田径训练时有人猛推了我一下，我扭伤了。——疼吗？——现在只是隐隐地疼。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1122 -->
# 四级单词 1122 · 荒谬的
中文：adj. 荒谬的，可笑的
单词：###ridiculous###
搭配：###a ridiculous idea### 荒唐的想法
例句：That talk show is ###ridiculous### — full of nonsense! — Agreed, and the vain host keeps boasting about himself.
译文：那个脱口秀太荒唐了——满是胡说八道！——同意，那个自负的主持人还一直自吹自擂。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1123 -->
# 四级单词 1123 · 胡说
中文：n. 胡说，废话
单词：###nonsense###
搭配：###talk nonsense### 胡说八道
例句：That talk show is ridiculous — full of ###nonsense###! — Agreed, and the vain host keeps boasting about himself.
译文：那个脱口秀太荒唐了——满是胡说八道！——同意，那个自负的主持人还一直自吹自擂。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1124 -->
# 四级单词 1124 · 自负的；徒劳的
中文：adj. 徒劳的；自负的
单词：###vain###
搭配：###in vain### 徒劳
例句：That talk show is ridiculous — full of nonsense! — Agreed, and the ###vain### host keeps boasting about himself.
译文：那个脱口秀太荒唐了——满是胡说八道！——同意，那个自负的主持人还一直自吹自擂。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1125 -->
# 四级单词 1125 · 自夸
中文：vi. 自夸 vt. 吹嘘
单词：###boast###
搭配：###boast about ...### 吹嘘……
例句：That talk show is ridiculous — full of nonsense! — Agreed, and the vain host keeps ###boasting### about himself.
译文：那个脱口秀太荒唐了——满是胡说八道！——同意，那个自负的主持人还一直自吹自擂。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1126 -->
# 四级单词 1126 · 结
中文：n. (绳的)结，(树的)节
单词：###knot###
搭配：###tie a knot### 打结
例句：Wait! The ###knot### on my shoe has loosened and come undone. — Hurry up, the queue for the bus is moving!
译文：等等！我鞋上的结松了，散开了。——快点，等公交的队伍在动了！
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1127 -->
# 四级单词 1127 · 松开
中文：vt. 解开；使松弛；松开
单词：###loosen###
搭配：###loosen one's tie### 松开领带
例句：Wait! The knot on my shoe has ###loosened### and come undone. — Hurry up, the queue for the bus is moving!
译文：等等！我鞋上的结松了，散开了。——快点，等公交的队伍在动了！
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1128 -->
# 四级单词 1128 · 解开；撤销
中文：vt. 解开，打开；取消；撤销
单词：###undo###
搭配：###come undone### 松开
例句：Wait! The knot on my shoe has loosened and come ###undone###. — Hurry up, the queue for the bus is moving!
译文：等等！我鞋上的结松了，散开了。——快点，等公交的队伍在动了！
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1129 -->
# 四级单词 1129 · 队；排队
中文：n. 行列 vi. 排队等候
单词：###queue###
搭配：###jump the queue### 插队
例句：Wait! The knot on my shoe has loosened and come undone. — Hurry up, the ###queue### for the bus is moving!
译文：等等！我鞋上的结松了，散开了。——快点，等公交的队伍在动了！
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1130 -->
# 四级单词 1130 · 任命
中文：vt. 任命，委任；约定
单词：###appoint###
搭配：###appoint sb. as ...### 任命某人为……
例句：Congratulations on being ###appointed### manager! — Thanks, but it's a heavy burden. Will you assist me with the quarterly report?
译文：祝贺你被任命为经理！——谢谢，可担子不轻。你能帮我做季度报告吗？
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1131 -->

中文：n. 担子，重担；装载量；负担
单词：###burden###
搭配：###a heavy burden### 沉重的负担
例句：Congratulations on being appointed manager! — Thanks, but it's a heavy ###burden###. Will you assist me with the quarterly report?
译文：祝贺你被任命为经理！——谢谢，可担子不轻。你能帮我做季度报告吗？
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1132 -->
# 四级单词 1132 · 帮助
中文：vt. 援助，帮助；搀扶
单词：###assist###
搭配：###assist sb. with sth.### 帮某人做某事
例句：Congratulations on being appointed manager! — Thanks, but it's a heavy burden. Will you ###assist### me with the quarterly report?
译文：祝贺你被任命为经理！——谢谢，可担子不轻。你能帮我做季度报告吗？
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1133 -->
# 四级单词 1133 · 季度的
中文：adj. 季度的 adv. 季度地
单词：###quarterly###
搭配：###a quarterly magazine### 季刊
例句：Congratulations on being appointed manager! — Thanks, but it's a heavy burden. Will you assist me with the ###quarterly### report?
译文：祝贺你被任命为经理！——谢谢，可担子不轻。你能帮我做季度报告吗？
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1134 -->
# 四级单词 1134 · 铁轨；栏杆
中文：n. 横条，横杆；铁轨；栏杆
单词：###rail###
搭配：###by rail### 乘火车
例句：Excuse me, how do I get to the station? — Follow the ###rail### track southeast, past the old tin factory, for approximately two kilometres.
译文：请问去车站怎么走？——沿着铁轨往东南走，经过那家旧锡厂，大约两公里。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1135 -->

单词：###southeast###
搭配：###the southeast coast### 东南沿海
例句：Excuse me, how do I get to the station? — Follow the rail track ###southeast###, past the old tin factory, for approximately two kilometres.
译文：请问去车站怎么走？——沿着铁轨往东南走，经过那家旧锡厂，大约两公里。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1136 -->
# 四级单词 1136 · 锡；罐头
中文：n. 锡；罐头
单词：###tin###
搭配：###a tin of beans### 一罐豆子
例句：Excuse me, how do I get to the station? — Follow the rail track southeast, past the old ###tin### factory, for approximately two kilometres.
译文：请问去车站怎么走？——沿着铁轨往东南走，经过那家旧锡厂，大约两公里。

搭配：###the realm of science### 科学领域
例句：Treated cruelly by landlords, the peasants rose up to overthrow them, and the regional rebellion soon spread throughout the ###realm###.
译文：农民们受地主残酷压迫，起来推翻他们，这场地方起义很快蔓延到了整个王国。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1775 -->

搭配：###a news bulletin### 新闻简报
例句：People once relied on printed ###bulletins###; today towers send out signals of various wavelengths, and every phone serves as an interactive receiver.

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1183 -->

搭配：###scatter seeds### 撒种子
例句：Tourists who ###scatter### useless plastic everywhere spoil our surroundings, and the plastic slowly kills birds and fish.

例句：Tourists who scatter ###useless### plastic everywhere spoil our surroundings, and the plastic slowly kills birds and fish.

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1192 -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1195 -->
# 四级单词 1195 · 团结；联合
中文：vi. 联合；团结 vt. 使联合
单词：###unite###
搭配：###unite against ...### 团结起来反对……
例句：A nation can only ###unite### and grow rich when wealth is shared fairly and the livelihood of the poor is not wholly ignored.
译文：只有财富分配公平、穷人的生计不被完全忽视，一个国家才能团结和富强。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1197 -->

搭配：###treat sb. fairly### 公平对待某人
例句：A nation can only unite and grow rich when wealth is shared ###fairly### and the livelihood of the poor is not wholly ignored.

<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1777 -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1198 -->

搭配：###wholly different### 完全不同

<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1199 -->

搭配：###a home remedy### 偏方
例句：This herb is the only ###remedy### that works when an infectious bite pierces the skin, according to the doctor's observation.

<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1778 -->

<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1203 -->

搭配：###specialize in ...### 专攻……
例句：The professor to whom I owe my interest in grammar ###specialized### in the grammatical usage of prepositions.
译文：我对语法的兴趣要归功于那位教授，他专门研究介词的语法用法。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

搭配：###a grammatical error### 语法错误

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1779 -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1211 -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1215 -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1217 -->

例句：Being true to oneself means doing what one believes is right and never letting unkind ###remarks### mislead one.

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1219 -->

中文：adj. 理论(上)的
单词：###theoretical###
搭配：###theoretical knowledge### 理论知识
例句：The answer to whether the ###theoretical### result can be repeated remains unknown, as the data are vague and partial.

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1220 -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1221 -->

中文：adj. 模糊的，含糊的
单词：###vague###
搭配：###a vague memory### 模糊的记忆
例句：The answer to whether the theoretical result can be repeated remains unknown, as the data are ###vague### and partial.
译文：这个理论结果能否重复验证，答案仍是未知数，因为数据模糊且不完整。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1222 -->

搭配：###construct a bridge### 建桥
例句：A railway connecting the northwest and the southwest has been ###constructed###, which has quickened economic growth.
译文：一条连接西北和西南的铁路已经建成，加快了经济发展。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1266 -->

例句：A railway connecting the northwest and the southwest has been constructed, which has ###quickened### economic growth.

<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1267 -->
# 四级单词 1267 · 马力
中文：n. 马力
单词：###horsepower###
搭配：###a 200-horsepower engine 200### 马力的发动机
例句：The new engine produces twice as much ###horsepower### as the old one, yet it is exceedingly economical and far less wasteful.
译文：新发动机的马力是旧发动机的两倍，却极其省油，浪费也少得多。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1268 -->

单词：###exceedingly###
搭配：###exceedingly difficult### 非常难
例句：The new engine produces twice as much horsepower as the old one, yet it is ###exceedingly### economical and far less wasteful.
译文：新发动机的马力是旧发动机的两倍，却极其省油，浪费也少得多。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1269 -->

单词：###economical###
搭配：###an economical car### 省油的车
例句：The new engine produces twice as much horsepower as the old one, yet it is exceedingly ###economical### and far less wasteful.
译文：新发动机的马力是旧发动机的两倍，却极其省油，浪费也少得多。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1789 -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1271 -->
# 四级单词 1271 · 自行车
中文：n. 自行车，脚踏车
单词：###bicycle###
搭配：###ride a bicycle### 骑自行车
例句：Of the two ###bicycles###, the lighter one is the more desirable, though its carrier is small and its bolts are loose.
译文：两辆自行车中，较轻的那辆更值得买，尽管它的后座架小、螺栓也松了。

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1272 -->

<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1273 -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1275 -->
# 四级单词 1275 · 疾病
中文：n. 疾病
单词：###sickness###
搭配：###motion sickness### 晕动症
例句：It was not until a long ###sickness### kept her in bed that she understood the significance of health and refrained from her habitual late nights.
译文：直到一场大病让她卧床不起，她才明白健康的重要，改掉了习惯性熬夜。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1276 -->

单词：###significance###
搭配：###of great significance### 意义重大
例句：It was not until a long sickness kept her in bed that she understood the ###significance### of health and refrained from her habitual late nights.
译文：直到一场大病让她卧床不起，她才明白健康的重要，改掉了习惯性熬夜。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1790 -->

译文：直到一场大病让她卧床不起，她才明白健康的重要，改掉了习惯性熬夜。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1278 -->

搭配：###a habitual liar### 惯于说谎的人

译文：直到一场大病让她卧床不起，她才明白健康的重要，改掉了习惯性熬夜。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1279 -->
# 四级单词 1279 · 招待；使娱乐

单词：###entertain###
搭配：###entertain guests### 招待客人
例句：Let's ###entertain### the new neighbours. Will you bake a cake? — Sure, with a cream crown! And let's invite the painter next door.
译文：我们来招待一下新邻居吧。你能烤个蛋糕吗？——好啊，顶上做一圈奶油！我们也请隔壁那位画家来吧。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1280 -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1284 -->

例句：Why so gloomy? — The boss handed me a ###handful### of overdue tasks, and unfortunately, refusal isn't an option.
译文：怎么这么沮丧？——老板塞给我一堆早该完成的任务，遗憾的是，我没法拒绝。

例句：Why so gloomy? — The boss handed me a handful of ###overdue### tasks, and unfortunately, refusal isn't an option.

例句：Do you sell petrol by the ###gallon###? — No, by the liter. We use the metric system, and temperatures are in centigrade.
译文：你们的汽油按加仑卖吗？——不，按升卖。我们用公制，温度用摄氏度。

例句：Do you sell petrol by the gallon? — No, by the ###liter###. We use the metric system, and temperatures are in centigrade.

搭配：###the metric system### 公制

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1792 -->
# 四级单词 1792 · 伙伴，好友（口语）

单词：###buddy###
搭配：###my old buddy### 老朋友
例句：Mum, a bee stung me! — Stop crying, ###buddy###, and don't suck the spot; this cream will soften the pain until it's tolerable.
译文：妈妈，蜜蜂蜇了我！——别哭了，宝贝，别吸那个地方；这个药膏能减轻疼痛，一会儿就没那么疼了。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1297 -->

<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1793 -->

<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1794 -->

<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1299 -->

译文：书架装好了吗？——木头框架装好了，可那些立体示意图把我看糊涂了。

搭配：###three-dimensional### 三维的，立体的

例句：Your oral English has improved a lot! — Thanks. I regularly ###retell### stories aloud and do a little translation.

搭配：###a word-for-word translation### 逐字翻译
例句：Your oral English has improved a lot! — Thanks. I regularly retell stories aloud and do a little ###translation###.
译文：你的英语口语进步很大！——谢谢。我经常大声复述故事，也做一点翻译。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1796 -->
# 四级单词 1796 · 锅炉；热水器
中文：n. 锅炉；热水器
单词：###boiler###
搭配：###a gas boiler### 燃气锅炉
例句：It's freezing and the ###boiler###'s broken! — I'll haul in a sack of wood. You kneel by the stove and blow until it burns.
译文：冻死了，锅炉还坏了！——我去拖一袋木柴进来。你跪在炉子边吹火，直到烧起来。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1797 -->

单词：###haul###
搭配：###haul in### 拖进来
例句：It's freezing and the boiler's broken! — I'll ###haul### in a sack of wood. You kneel by the stove and blow until it burns.
译文：冻死了，锅炉还坏了！——我去拖一袋木柴进来。你跪在炉子边吹火，直到烧起来。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

例句：It's freezing and the boiler's broken! — I'll haul in a ###sack### of wood. You kneel by the stove and blow until it burns.

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1311 -->
# 四级单词 1311 · 喷气式飞机
中文：n. 喷气式飞机；喷嘴
单词：###jet###
搭配：###a jet plane### 喷气式飞机
例句：A ###jet### vanished from the radar last night. — Is there any possibility it landed safely?
译文：昨晚一架喷气式飞机从雷达上消失了。——它有没有可能安全降落了？
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1312 -->

搭配：###on the radar### 在雷达上
例句：A jet vanished from the ###radar### last night. — Is there any possibility it landed safely?
译文：昨晚一架喷气式飞机从雷达上消失了。——它有没有可能安全降落了？
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1313 -->

中文：n. 可能；可能的事；可能性
单词：###possibility###
搭配：###a strong possibility### 很大的可能
例句：A jet vanished from the radar last night. — Is there any ###possibility### it landed safely?
译文：昨晚一架喷气式飞机从雷达上消失了。——它有没有可能安全降落了？
<!-- /leiji-note -->

搭配：###arrive safely### 平安到达
例句：A jet vanished from the radar last night. — Is there any possibility it landed ###safely###?

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1315 -->
# 四级单词 1315 · 钩子
中文：n. 钩，挂钩；钩子 vt. 钩住
单词：###hook###
搭配：###hang ... on a hook### 把……挂在钩上
例句：Help me hang this mirror? — Sure. Fix the ###hook###, put the string over it, move it upward and straighten it until it's vertical.
译文：帮我挂一下这面镜子好吗？——好。把钩子固定好，把绳子挂上去，往上提，再扶正，直到它竖直。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1317 -->

搭配：###move upward### 向上移

<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1798 -->

搭配：###straighten up### 挺直身子
例句：Help me hang this mirror? — Sure. Fix the hook, put the string over it, move it upward and ###straighten### it until it's vertical.
译文：帮我挂一下这面镜子好吗？——好。把钩子固定好，把绳子挂上去，往上提，再扶正，直到它竖直。

搭配：###a vertical line### 竖线
例句：Help me hang this mirror? — Sure. Fix the hook, put the string over it, move it upward and straighten it until it's ###vertical###.

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1799 -->
# 四级单词 1799 · 新奇，新鲜事物
中文：n. 新颖；新颖的事物；新颖而价廉的小物件；新奇
单词：###novelty###
搭配：###the novelty of### 的新鲜感
例句：Come and look! The hen hatched baby chickens in soft feathers — a ###novelty### for the kids — and the cock stands guardian over them.
译文：快来看！母鸡孵出了毛茸茸的小鸡——孩子们觉得可新鲜了——公鸡守在一旁护着它们。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1321 -->

搭配：###as light as a feather### 轻如鸿毛
例句：Come and look! The hen hatched baby chickens in soft ###feathers### — a novelty for the kids — and the cock stands guardian over them.
译文：快来看！母鸡孵出了毛茸茸的小鸡——孩子们觉得可新鲜了——公鸡守在一旁护着它们。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1322 -->

单词：###cock###
搭配：###a fighting cock### 斗鸡
例句：Come and look! The hen hatched baby chickens in soft feathers — a novelty for the kids — and the ###cock### stands guardian over them.
译文：快来看！母鸡孵出了毛茸茸的小鸡——孩子们觉得可新鲜了——公鸡守在一旁护着它们。

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1800 -->

单词：###guardian###
搭配：###a legal guardian### 法定监护人
例句：Come and look! The hen hatched baby chickens in soft feathers — a novelty for the kids — and the cock stands ###guardian### over them.

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1323 -->
# 四级单词 1323 · 爱好者
中文：n. 爱好者；情人；情侣
单词：###lover###
搭配：###a music lover### 音乐爱好者
例句：I've just bought my first car! — You car ###lover###! Which manufacturer? — A local one. — Insure it; you can't possibly drive without cover.
译文：我刚买了第一辆车！——你这个车迷！哪个厂家的？——本地的。——去上保险吧，没保险可不能开。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1324 -->

中文：n. 制造商/厂
单词：###manufacturer###
搭配：###a car manufacturer### 汽车制造商
例句：I've just bought my first car! — You car lover! Which ###manufacturer###? — A local one. — Insure it; you can't possibly drive without cover.
译文：我刚买了第一辆车！——你这个车迷！哪个厂家的？——本地的。——去上保险吧，没保险可不能开。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1325 -->
# 四级单词 1325 · 给……保险
中文：vt. 给…保险；确保
单词：###insure###
搭配：###insure a house### 给房子投保
例句：I've just bought my first car! — You car lover! Which manufacturer? — A local one. — ###Insure### it; you can't possibly drive without cover.
译文：我刚买了第一辆车！——你这个车迷！哪个厂家的？——本地的。——去上保险吧，没保险可不能开。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1326 -->

中文：adv. 可能地；也许；无论如何
单词：###possibly###
搭配：###cannot possibly### 绝不可能
例句：I've just bought my first car! — You car lover! Which manufacturer? — A local one. — Insure it; you can't ###possibly### drive without cover.
译文：我刚买了第一辆车！——你这个车迷！哪个厂家的？——本地的。——去上保险吧，没保险可不能开。

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1327 -->
# 四级单词 1327 · 使明亮
中文：vt. 照亮；减轻；使明亮
单词：###lighten###
搭配：###lighten the room### 使房间明亮
例句：What lovely roses! They ###lighten### the whole room. — Thanks. They're still wet with dew; cut the stems and they'll bloom for days.
译文：多漂亮的玫瑰！把屋子都照亮了。——谢谢。它们还带着露水，把花茎剪一下，能开好几天。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1328 -->

单词：###dew###
搭配：###morning dew### 晨露
例句：What lovely roses! They lighten the whole room. — Thanks. They're still wet with ###dew###; cut the stems and they'll bloom for days.
译文：多漂亮的玫瑰！把屋子都照亮了。——谢谢。它们还带着露水，把花茎剪一下，能开好几天。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1329 -->

例句：What lovely roses! They lighten the whole room. — Thanks. They're still wet with dew; cut the ###stems### and they'll bloom for days.

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1330 -->

搭配：###in full bloom### 盛开
例句：What lovely roses! They lighten the whole room. — Thanks. They're still wet with dew; cut the stems and they'll ###bloom### for days.

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1331 -->
# 四级单词 1331 · 部分地

搭配：###partly true### 部分正确
例句：This machine keeps jamming! — The paper is ###partly### stuck. Check the roller, and feed sheets of different thickness separately.
译文：这台机器老卡纸！——纸有一部分卡住了。检查一下滚筒，不同厚度的纸分开放。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1332 -->

单词：###roller###
搭配：###a paint roller### 油漆滚筒
例句：This machine keeps jamming! — The paper is partly stuck. Check the ###roller###, and feed sheets of different thickness separately.
译文：这台机器老卡纸！——纸有一部分卡住了。检查一下滚筒，不同厚度的纸分开放。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1333 -->

中文：n. 厚(度)；密(度)
单词：###thickness###
搭配：###the thickness of the wall### 墙的厚度
例句：This machine keeps jamming! — The paper is partly stuck. Check the roller, and feed sheets of different ###thickness### separately.
译文：这台机器老卡纸！——纸有一部分卡住了。检查一下滚筒，不同厚度的纸分开放。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1334 -->

中文：adv. 分离地；分别地
单词：###separately###
搭配：###pay separately### 分开付钱
例句：This machine keeps jamming! — The paper is partly stuck. Check the roller, and feed sheets of different thickness ###separately###.
译文：这台机器老卡纸！——纸有一部分卡住了。检查一下滚筒，不同厚度的纸分开放。

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1335 -->
# 四级单词 1335 · 某个时候
中文：adv. 在某一时候；从前
单词：###sometime###
搭配：###sometime next week### 下周某个时候
例句：Let's meet ###sometime### next week. — Sure. Friday is probable, but it isn't definite yet; dial my office tomorrow.
译文：下周找个时间见个面吧。——好啊。很可能是周五，但还没定下来，明天打我办公室电话吧。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1336 -->
# 四级单词 1336 · 很可能的
中文：adj. 很可能的，大概的
单词：###probable###
搭配：###a probable result### 可能的结果
例句：Let's meet sometime next week. — Sure. Friday is ###probable###, but it isn't definite yet; dial my office tomorrow.
译文：下周找个时间见个面吧。——好啊。很可能是周五，但还没定下来，明天打我办公室电话吧。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1337 -->
# 四级单词 1337 · 明确的
中文：adj. 明确的；肯定的
单词：###definite###
搭配：###a definite answer### 明确的答复
例句：Let's meet sometime next week. — Sure. Friday is probable, but it isn't ###definite### yet; dial my office tomorrow.
译文：下周找个时间见个面吧。——好啊。很可能是周五，但还没定下来，明天打我办公室电话吧。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1338 -->
# 四级单词 1338 · 拨（号）
中文：n. 钟面；拨号盘 vt. 拨
单词：###dial###
搭配：###dial a number### 拨号码
例句：Let's meet sometime next week. — Sure. Friday is probable, but it isn't definite yet; ###dial### my office tomorrow.
译文：下周找个时间见个面吧。——好啊。很可能是周五，但还没定下来，明天打我办公室电话吧。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1339 -->
# 四级单词 1339 · 不同意
中文：vi. 有分歧；不一致；不同意
单词：###disagree###
搭配：###disagree with sb.### 不同意某人
例句：I ###disagree### with the view that failure is purely harmful; with few exceptions, it offers valuable, if indirect, lessons.
译文：我不同意失败只有坏处这个观点；除了少数例外，它能带来宝贵的教训，哪怕是间接的。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1340 -->

中文：adv. 纯粹地，完全地
单词：###purely###
搭配：###purely by chance### 纯属偶然
例句：I disagree with the view that failure is ###purely### harmful; with few exceptions, it offers valuable, if indirect, lessons.
译文：我不同意失败只有坏处这个观点；除了少数例外，它能带来宝贵的教训，哪怕是间接的。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1341 -->
# 四级单词 1341 · 例外
中文：n. 例外，除外
单词：###exception###
搭配：###without exception### 无一例外
例句：I disagree with the view that failure is purely harmful; with few ###exceptions###, it offers valuable, if indirect, lessons.
译文：我不同意失败只有坏处这个观点；除了少数例外，它能带来宝贵的教训，哪怕是间接的。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1342 -->
# 四级单词 1342 · 间接的
中文：adj. 间接的；不坦率的
单词：###indirect###
搭配：###an indirect answer### 间接的回答
例句：I disagree with the view that failure is purely harmful; with few exceptions, it offers valuable, if ###indirect###, lessons.
译文：我不同意失败只有坏处这个观点；除了少数例外，它能带来宝贵的教训，哪怕是间接的。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1343 -->
# 四级单词 1343 · 进步的
中文：adj. 进步/向前进的
单词：###progressive###
搭配：###progressive ideas### 进步思想
例句：A ###progressive### society must condemn slavery in any form and liberate those who are still treated as property.
译文：一个进步的社会必须谴责任何形式的奴役，解放那些仍被当作财产对待的人。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1344 -->

单词：###condemn###
搭配：###condemn violence### 谴责暴力
例句：A progressive society must ###condemn### slavery in any form and liberate those who are still treated as property.
译文：一个进步的社会必须谴责任何形式的奴役，解放那些仍被当作财产对待的人。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1345 -->

中文：n. 奴隶制度；苦役；奴役
单词：###slavery###
搭配：###abolish slavery### 废除奴隶制
例句：A progressive society must condemn ###slavery### in any form and liberate those who are still treated as property.
译文：一个进步的社会必须谴责任何形式的奴役，解放那些仍被当作财产对待的人。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1346 -->

中文：vt. 解放；释放
单词：###liberate###
搭配：###liberate a city### 解放一座城市
例句：A progressive society must condemn slavery in any form and ###liberate### those who are still treated as property.
译文：一个进步的社会必须谴责任何形式的奴役，解放那些仍被当作财产对待的人。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1347 -->
# 四级单词 1347 · 缺点；不利
中文：n. 不利(地位)；缺点
单词：###disadvantage###
搭配：###at a disadvantage### 处于不利地位

<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1388 -->
# 四级单词 1388 · 偶尔
中文：adv. 偶然；非经常地；偶尔
单词：###occasionally###
搭配：###only occasionally### 只是偶尔
例句：Bright screens tire our eyes, so as a means of prevention we should ###occasionally### gaze at distant objects or walk outdoors.
译文：明亮的屏幕让眼睛疲劳，所以为了预防，我们应该时常远眺或到户外走走。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1389 -->

# 四级单词 1399 · 无知的
中文：adj. 不知道的；无知的

搭配：###be ignorant of ...### 不知道……
例句：Only ###ignorant### people trust such slogans as promise unlikely results, like losing twenty percent of one's weight in a week.
译文：只有无知的人才会相信那些承诺不可能结果的口号，比如一周减掉百分之二十的体重。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1809 -->
# 四级单词 1809 · 评论；解说

单词：###commentary###
搭配：###a running commentary### 现场解说
例句：In his ###commentary###, the fortunate correspondent described with photographic detail the people and scenery that he had seen on that tropical island.

<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1411 -->

搭配：###the theory of relativity### 相对论
例句：###Relativity### shook the absolute certainty that time bears no relation to motion, a belief central to classical mechanics.
译文：相对论动摇了人们绝对确信的“时间与运动无关”这一观点，而这正是经典力学的核心信念。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1811 -->
# 四级单词 1811 · 有道德的，品德高尚的
中文：adj. 有道德的，品行端正的，品德高的；自命清高的，自以为是的
单词：###virtuous###
搭配：###a virtuous life### 高尚的生活
例句：Tortured in prison, the general would not give in, nor would he betray his men, for he was a ###virtuous### and honourable soldier.
译文：那位将军在狱中受尽折磨也不屈服，更不出卖部下，因为他是一个品德高尚、光明磊落的军人。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1431 -->
# 四级单词 1431 · 光荣的；正直的
中文：adj. 诚实的；光荣的；正直的
单词：###honourable###
搭配：###an honourable man### 正人君子
例句：Tortured in prison, the general would not give in, nor would he betray his men, for he was a virtuous and ###honourable### soldier.
译文：那位将军在狱中受尽折磨也不屈服，更不出卖部下，因为他是一个品德高尚、光明磊落的军人。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1432 -->
# 四级单词 1432 · 成功地
中文：adv. 成功地
单词：###successfully###
搭配：###successfully complete### 顺利完成
例句：Only by working overtime did the factory ###successfully### complete production and fill its storage with stock before the holiday.
译文：工厂只有靠加班，才在假期前顺利完成生产并把仓库备足了货。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1433 -->

中文：n. 生产；产品；总产量
单词：###production###
搭配：###mass production### 大规模生产
例句：Only by working overtime did the factory successfully complete ###production### and fill its storage with stock before the holiday.
译文：工厂只有靠加班，才在假期前顺利完成生产并把仓库备足了货。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1434 -->

中文：n. 贮藏；贮藏量；储存
单词：###storage###
搭配：###in storage### 在储存中
例句：Only by working overtime did the factory successfully complete production and fill its ###storage### with stock before the holiday.
译文：工厂只有靠加班，才在假期前顺利完成生产并把仓库备足了货。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1435 -->

中文：n. 原料；库存品；股本；股票
单词：###stock###
搭配：###in stock### 有现货
例句：Only by working overtime did the factory successfully complete production and fill its storage with ###stock### before the holiday.
译文：工厂只有靠加班，才在假期前顺利完成生产并把仓库备足了货。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1436 -->
# 四级单词 1436 · 丰富的
中文：adj. 丰富的；大量的
单词：###abundant###
搭配：###abundant resources### 丰富的资源
例句：Nowhere else can you find such ###abundant### herbs and lively trade; items cost only pennies, whilst dealers chat cheerfully.
译文：别处再也找不到这么丰富的草药和这么热闹的买卖；东西只要几便士，商贩们则在一旁乐呵呵地聊天。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1438 -->

中文：n. 便士；分
单词：###penny###
搭配：###cost a few pennies### 只值几便士
例句：Nowhere else can you find such abundant herbs and lively trade; items cost only ###pennies###, whilst dealers chat cheerfully.
译文：别处再也找不到这么丰富的草药和这么热闹的买卖；东西只要几便士，商贩们则在一旁乐呵呵地聊天。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1439 -->
# 四级单词 1439 · 当……时；而
中文：conj. 当…的时候；而
单词：###whilst###
搭配：###whilst waiting### 在等的时候
例句：Nowhere else can you find such abundant herbs and lively trade; items cost only pennies, ###whilst### dealers chat cheerfully.
译文：别处再也找不到这么丰富的草药和这么热闹的买卖；东西只要几便士，商贩们则在一旁乐呵呵地聊天。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1812 -->

中文：n. 商人；发牌者
单词：###dealer###
搭配：###a car dealer### 汽车经销商
例句：Nowhere else can you find such abundant herbs and lively trade; items cost only pennies, whilst ###dealers### chat cheerfully.
译文：别处再也找不到这么丰富的草药和这么热闹的买卖；东西只要几便士，商贩们则在一旁乐呵呵地聊天。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1440 -->
# 四级单词 1440 · 猛烈的；暴力的
中文：adj. 猛烈的；狂暴的；暴力的
单词：###violent###
搭配：###violent crime### 暴力犯罪
例句：Having been warned of the ###violent### storm, the scouts sharpened their stakes and drove them deep into the ground.
译文：童子军们得到暴风雨猛烈的警告后，削尖了木桩，把它们深深打进地里。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1441 -->

单词：###scout###
搭配：###a boy scout### 童子军
例句：Having been warned of the violent storm, the ###scouts### sharpened their stakes and drove them deep into the ground.
译文：童子军们得到暴风雨猛烈的警告后，削尖了木桩，把它们深深打进地里。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1442 -->

单词：###sharpen###
搭配：###sharpen a pencil### 削铅笔
例句：Having been warned of the violent storm, the scouts ###sharpened### their stakes and drove them deep into the ground.
译文：童子军们得到暴风雨猛烈的警告后，削尖了木桩，把它们深深打进地里。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1443 -->
# 四级单词 1443 · 木桩；赌注
中文：n. 桩；赌金；奖品；木桩；赌注
单词：###stake###
搭配：###at stake### 处于危险中
例句：Having been warned of the violent storm, the scouts sharpened their ###stakes### and drove them deep into the ground.
译文：童子军们得到暴风雨猛烈的警告后，削尖了木桩，把它们深深打进地里。

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1444 -->
# 四级单词 1444 · 设计；发明
中文：vt. 设计，发明
单词：###devise###
搭配：###devise a plan### 想出计划
例句：The engineer ###devised### a new crane, only to learn that his counterpart at a rival firm had built a similar one, which left him dissatisfied.
译文：工程师设计出一台新起重机，结果却得知对手公司的同行已经造了一台类似的，这让他很不满意。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1446 -->
# 四级单词 1446 · 起重机；鹤
中文：n. 起重机，摄影升降机；鹤
单词：###crane###
搭配：###a tower crane### 塔吊
例句：The engineer devised a new ###crane###, only to learn that his counterpart at a rival firm had built a similar one, which left him dissatisfied.
译文：工程师设计出一台新起重机，结果却得知对手公司的同行已经造了一台类似的，这让他很不满意。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1813 -->

中文：n. 相对应的人(物)；职位相当的人
单词：###counterpart###
搭配：###his counterpart### 与他职位相当的人
例句：The engineer devised a new crane, only to learn that his ###counterpart### at a rival firm had built a similar one, which left him dissatisfied.
译文：工程师设计出一台新起重机，结果却得知对手公司的同行已经造了一台类似的，这让他很不满意。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1447 -->
# 四级单词 1447 · 使不满
中文：vt. 使不满，使不平
单词：###dissatisfy###
搭配：###be dissatisfied with ...### 对……不满
例句：The engineer devised a new crane, only to learn that his counterpart at a rival firm had built a similar one, which left him ###dissatisfied###.
译文：工程师设计出一台新起重机，结果却得知对手公司的同行已经造了一台类似的，这让他很不满意。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1814 -->
# 四级单词 1814 · 危险的，有害的
中文：adj. 危险的；有害的
单词：###hazardous###
搭配：###hazardous waste### 有害废料
例句：Avoid being exposed to ###hazardous### chemicals at work, as their absorption may permanently disable vital organs.
译文：工作时避免接触危险化学品，因为身体吸收后可能会永久损伤重要器官。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1815 -->

中文：n. 吸收；专注
单词：###absorption###
搭配：###the absorption of water### 水的吸收
例句：Avoid being exposed to hazardous chemicals at work, as their ###absorption### may permanently disable vital organs.
译文：工作时避免接触危险化学品，因为身体吸收后可能会永久损伤重要器官。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1450 -->

中文：adv. 永久/持久地
单词：###permanently###
搭配：###be permanently damaged### 永久受损
例句：Avoid being exposed to hazardous chemicals at work, as their absorption may ###permanently### disable vital organs.
译文：工作时避免接触危险化学品，因为身体吸收后可能会永久损伤重要器官。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1451 -->
# 四级单词 1451 · 使丧失能力
中文：vt. 使无能，使伤残；使丧失能力
单词：###disable###
搭配：###be disabled by ...### 因……致残
例句：Avoid being exposed to hazardous chemicals at work, as their absorption may permanently ###disable### vital organs.
译文：工作时避免接触危险化学品，因为身体吸收后可能会永久损伤重要器官。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1452 -->
# 四级单词 1452 · 蜡
中文：n. 蜡，蜂蜡
单词：###wax###
搭配：###a wax candle### 蜡烛
例句：This letter can't be genuine; the ###wax### seal looks too fresh and the surname is incorrect.
译文：这封信不可能是真的；蜡封看起来太新，姓氏也写错了。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1453 -->

中文：n. 封蜡；印记；封印；海豹 vt. 封
单词：###seal###
搭配：###a wax seal### 蜡封
例句：This letter can't be genuine; the wax ###seal### looks too fresh and the surname is incorrect.
译文：这封信不可能是真的；蜡封看起来太新，姓氏也写错了。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1454 -->

中文：n. 姓
单词：###surname###
搭配：###family surname### 姓氏
例句：This letter can't be genuine; the wax seal looks too fresh and the ###surname### is incorrect.
译文：这封信不可能是真的；蜡封看起来太新，姓氏也写错了。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1455 -->
# 四级单词 1455 · 不正确的
中文：adj. 不正确的，错误的
单词：###incorrect###
搭配：###an incorrect answer### 错误答案
例句：This letter can't be genuine; the wax seal looks too fresh and the surname is ###incorrect###.
译文：这封信不可能是真的；蜡封看起来太新，姓氏也写错了。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1456 -->
# 四级单词 1456 · 到达
中文：n. 到达；到来；到达者
单词：###arrival###
搭配：###on arrival### 到达时
例句：The ship's ###arrival### is at noon after an intermediate stop; it leaves at six, so arrange your sightseeing accordingly.
译文：船中午到，途中停靠一站；它六点开，所以请相应安排你的观光。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1457 -->
# 四级单词 1457 · 中间的；中级的
中文：adj. 中间/级的
单词：###intermediate###
搭配：###an intermediate stop### 中途停靠站
例句：The ship's arrival is at noon after an ###intermediate### stop; it leaves at six, so arrange your sightseeing accordingly.
译文：船中午到，途中停靠一站；它六点开，所以请相应安排你的观光。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1458 -->
# 四级单词 1458 · 安排
中文：vt. 筹备；整理；调解；安排
单词：###arrange###
搭配：###arrange a meeting### 安排会议
例句：The ship's arrival is at noon after an intermediate stop; it leaves at six, so ###arrange### your sightseeing accordingly.
译文：船中午到，途中停靠一站；它六点开，所以请相应安排你的观光。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1459 -->
# 四级单词 1459 · 相应地
中文：adv. 因此，所以；照着；相应地
单词：###accordingly###
搭配：###act accordingly### 照着做
例句：The ship's arrival is at noon after an intermediate stop; it leaves at six, so arrange your sightseeing ###accordingly###.
译文：船中午到，途中停靠一站；它六点开，所以请相应安排你的观光。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1460 -->
# 四级单词 1460 · 武断的；任意的
中文：adj. 随心所欲的；专断的；武断的；任意的
单词：###arbitrary###
搭配：###an arbitrary decision### 武断的决定
例句：The minister, as well as his assistants, was accused of ###arbitrary### decisions on waste disposal and held accountable by legislators.
译文：部长和他的助手们被指控在垃圾处理上作出了武断的决定，并被议员们问责。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1461 -->
# 四级单词 1461 · 处理
中文：n. 丢掉，处理，销毁
单词：###disposal###
搭配：###waste disposal### 垃圾处理
例句：The minister, as well as his assistants, was accused of arbitrary decisions on waste ###disposal### and held accountable by legislators.
译文：部长和他的助手们被指控在垃圾处理上作出了武断的决定，并被议员们问责。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1816 -->
# 四级单词 1816 · 负有责任的
中文：adj. 应负责的，有责任的
单词：###accountable###
搭配：###be held accountable for### 对……负责
例句：The minister, as well as his assistants, was accused of arbitrary decisions on waste disposal and held ###accountable### by legislators.
译文：部长和他的助手们被指控在垃圾处理上作出了武断的决定，并被议员们问责。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1817 -->
# 四级单词 1817 · 立法者，议员
中文：n. 立法者；议员
单词：###legislator###
搭配：###state legislators### 州议员
例句：The minister, as well as his assistants, was accused of arbitrary decisions on waste disposal and held accountable by ###legislators###.
译文：部长和他的助手们被指控在垃圾处理上作出了武断的决定，并被议员们问责。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1464 -->
# 四级单词 1464 · 害怕的
中文：adj. 害怕的，可怕的
单词：###fearful###
搭配：###be fearful of ...### 害怕……
例句：The ###fearful### boy's ghosts were more imaginary than real, yet the suspense of each night, stirred by his imaginative mind, kept him awake.
译文：那个胆小男孩的鬼魂与其说是真的，不如说是想象出来的，可他丰富的想象力让每个夜晚都提心吊胆，使他难以入睡。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1465 -->
# 四级单词 1465 · 想象中的
中文：adj. 想象中的，假想的
单词：###imaginary###
搭配：###an imaginary friend### 想象中的朋友
例句：The fearful boy's ghosts were more ###imaginary### than real, yet the suspense of each night, stirred by his imaginative mind, kept him awake.
译文：那个胆小男孩的鬼魂与其说是真的，不如说是想象出来的，可他丰富的想象力让每个夜晚都提心吊胆，使他难以入睡。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1818 -->
# 四级单词 1818 · 悬念；挂虑
中文：n. 担心，焦虑；悬念，不确定；挂虑
单词：###suspense###
搭配：###keep sb. in suspense### 让某人悬着心
例句：The fearful boy's ghosts were more imaginary than real, yet the ###suspense### of each night, stirred by his imaginative mind, kept him awake.
译文：那个胆小男孩的鬼魂与其说是真的，不如说是想象出来的，可他丰富的想象力让每个夜晚都提心吊胆，使他难以入睡。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1819 -->
# 四级单词 1819 · 富有想象力的
中文：adj. 富于想像力的，想像的；富有想象力的
单词：###imaginative###
搭配：###an imaginative writer### 富有想象力的作家
例句：The fearful boy's ghosts were more imaginary than real, yet the suspense of each night, stirred by his ###imaginative### mind, kept him awake.
译文：那个胆小男孩的鬼魂与其说是真的，不如说是想象出来的，可他丰富的想象力让每个夜晚都提心吊胆，使他难以入睡。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1820 -->
# 四级单词 1820 · 可敬的，体面的
中文：adj. 可敬的；人格高尚的；体面的
单词：###respectable###
搭配：###a respectable family### 体面的家庭
例句：It is strange that such a ###respectable### priest, with a clear conscience, should act as if troubled by sin and the devil.
译文：奇怪的是，这样一位受人尊敬、问心无愧的牧师，竟然表现得好像被罪孽和魔鬼困扰着一样。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1469 -->
# 四级单词 1469 · 良心
中文：n. 良心，道德心
单词：###conscience###
搭配：###a clear conscience### 问心无愧
例句：It is strange that such a respectable priest, with a clear ###conscience###, should act as if troubled by sin and the devil.
译文：奇怪的是，这样一位受人尊敬、问心无愧的牧师，竟然表现得好像被罪孽和魔鬼困扰着一样。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1470 -->
# 四级单词 1470 · 罪；罪孽
中文：n. 罪，罪孽 vi. 犯罪
单词：###sin###
搭配：###commit a sin### 犯罪
例句：It is strange that such a respectable priest, with a clear conscience, should act as if troubled by ###sin### and the devil.
译文：奇怪的是，这样一位受人尊敬、问心无愧的牧师，竟然表现得好像被罪孽和魔鬼困扰着一样。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1471 -->
# 四级单词 1471 · 魔鬼
中文：n. 魔鬼，恶魔
单词：###devil###
搭配：###the devil### 魔鬼
例句：It is strange that such a respectable priest, with a clear conscience, should act as if troubled by sin and the ###devil###.
译文：奇怪的是，这样一位受人尊敬、问心无愧的牧师，竟然表现得好像被罪孽和魔鬼困扰着一样。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1472 -->
# 四级单词 1472 · 紧张的；时态
中文：n. 时态，时 adj. 紧张的
单词：###tense###
搭配：###feel tense### 感到紧张
例句：I'm so ###tense### before the exam! — Relax. First, read carefully; secondly, underline key words; thirdly, check your answers.
译文：考试前我好紧张！——放松。第一，仔细读题；第二，划出关键词；第三，检查答案。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1473 -->

单词：###secondly###
搭配：###Secondly, ...### 其次，……
例句：I'm so tense before the exam! — Relax. First, read carefully; ###secondly###, underline key words; thirdly, check your answers.
译文：考试前我好紧张！——放松。第一，仔细读题；第二，划出关键词；第三，检查答案。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1474 -->

搭配：###underline the key words### 划出关键词
例句：I'm so tense before the exam! — Relax. First, read carefully; secondly, ###underline### key words; thirdly, check your answers.
译文：考试前我好紧张！——放松。第一，仔细读题；第二，划出关键词；第三，检查答案。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1476 -->
# 四级单词 1476 · 缩水；收缩
中文：vi. 收缩；缩小；退缩；缩水
单词：###shrink###
搭配：###shrink in the wash### 洗后缩水
例句：Oh no, my sweater ###shrank### in the wash! — That powder is unsuitable for wool; always read the clothing label first.
译文：糟了，我的毛衣洗缩水了！——那种洗衣粉不适合羊毛，一定要先看衣服上的标签。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1477 -->

例句：Oh no, my sweater shrank in the wash! — That ###powder### is unsuitable for wool; always read the clothing label first.

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1480 -->
# 四级单词 1480 · 旋转；脱水
中文：vt. 纺；使旋转；脱水 n. 旋转
单词：###spin###
搭配：###spin around### 转身
例句：The washing machine won't ###spin###, and I don't know the exact cause. — Here's a hint: it's too full. Get rid of some towels.
译文：洗衣机不脱水，我也不知道确切原因。——给你个提示：东西放太多了。拿出几条毛巾。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1481 -->

搭配：###the exact time### 准确时间
例句：The washing machine won't spin, and I don't know the ###exact### cause. — Here's a hint: it's too full. Get rid of some towels.
译文：洗衣机不脱水，我也不知道确切原因。——给你个提示：东西放太多了。拿出几条毛巾。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1482 -->

单词：###hint###
搭配：###give a hint### 给个提示
例句：The washing machine won't spin, and I don't know the exact cause. — Here's a ###hint###: it's too full. Get rid of some towels.
译文：洗衣机不脱水，我也不知道确切原因。——给你个提示：东西放太多了。拿出几条毛巾。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1483 -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1821 -->
# 四级单词 1821 · 后院
中文：n. 后院
单词：###backyard###
搭配：###in the backyard### 在后院
例句：Have the strawberries in the ###backyard### ripened? — Some have. Let's pick them, irrigate the rest and clear away the junk.
译文：后院的草莓熟了吗？——有些熟了。我们去摘了，给剩下的浇水，再把杂物清掉。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1484 -->

搭配：###ripen in the sun### 在阳光下成熟
例句：Have the strawberries in the backyard ###ripened###? — Some have. Let's pick them, irrigate the rest and clear away the junk.
译文：后院的草莓熟了吗？——有些熟了。我们去摘了，给剩下的浇水，再把杂物清掉。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1822 -->

单词：###irrigate###
搭配：###irrigate the fields### 灌溉农田
例句：Have the strawberries in the backyard ripened? — Some have. Let's pick them, ###irrigate### the rest and clear away the junk.
译文：后院的草莓熟了吗？——有些熟了。我们去摘了，给剩下的浇水，再把杂物清掉。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1823 -->

单词：###junk###
搭配：###junk food### 垃圾食品
例句：Have the strawberries in the backyard ripened? — Some have. Let's pick them, irrigate the rest and clear away the ###junk###.
译文：后院的草莓熟了吗？——有些熟了。我们去摘了，给剩下的浇水，再把杂物清掉。

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1488 -->
# 四级单词 1488 · 发誓；咒骂
中文：vt. 宣(誓)；发誓；咒骂 vi. 诅咒
单词：###swear###
搭配：###swear to God### 对天发誓
例句：Do you ###swear### you had nothing to do with my cake's disappearance? — I swear! — Your denial is weak; there's cream wherever you've walked.
译文：你敢发誓我的蛋糕不见了和你无关吗？——我发誓！——你的否认太没说服力了，你走过的地方都有奶油。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1824 -->

单词：###disappearance###
搭配：###a mysterious disappearance### 神秘失踪
例句：Do you swear you had nothing to do with my cake's ###disappearance###? — I swear! — Your denial is weak; there's cream wherever you've walked.
译文：你敢发誓我的蛋糕不见了和你无关吗？——我发誓！——你的否认太没说服力了，你走过的地方都有奶油。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1825 -->

中文：n. 否认，拒绝，抛弃
单词：###denial###
搭配：###a flat denial### 断然否认
例句：Do you swear you had nothing to do with my cake's disappearance? — I swear! — Your ###denial### is weak; there's cream wherever you've walked.
译文：你敢发誓我的蛋糕不见了和你无关吗？——我发誓！——你的否认太没说服力了，你走过的地方都有奶油。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1491 -->

中文：adv. 无论在哪里
单词：###wherever###
搭配：###wherever you go### 无论你去哪儿
例句：Do you swear you had nothing to do with my cake's disappearance? — I swear! — Your denial is weak; there's cream ###wherever### you've walked.
译文：你敢发誓我的蛋糕不见了和你无关吗？——我发誓！——你的否认太没说服力了，你走过的地方都有奶油。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1492 -->
# 四级单词 1492 · 忽视；俯瞰
中文：vt. 眺望；看漏；放任；忽视；俯瞰
单词：###overlook###
搭配：###overlook a mistake### 忽视错误
例句：Sorry, I ###overlooked### your email. — No problem, but you omitted the newly updated file; please send a correction by Friday.
译文：抱歉，我漏看了你的邮件。——没关系，不过你漏发了刚更新的文件，请在周五前发个更正。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1493 -->

中文：vt. 省略，省去；遗漏
单词：###omit###
搭配：###omit a word### 漏掉一个词
例句：Sorry, I overlooked your email. — No problem, but you ###omitted### the newly updated file; please send a correction by Friday.
译文：抱歉，我漏看了你的邮件。——没关系，不过你漏发了刚更新的文件，请在周五前发个更正。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1494 -->

中文：adv. 新近，最近
单词：###newly###
搭配：###newly built### 新建的
例句：Sorry, I overlooked your email. — No problem, but you omitted the ###newly### updated file; please send a correction by Friday.
译文：抱歉，我漏看了你的邮件。——没关系，不过你漏发了刚更新的文件，请在周五前发个更正。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1495 -->

中文：n. 改正，纠正，修改
单词：###correction###
搭配：###make corrections### 改正
例句：Sorry, I overlooked your email. — No problem, but you omitted the newly updated file; please send a ###correction### by Friday.
译文：抱歉，我漏看了你的邮件。——没关系，不过你漏发了刚更新的文件，请在周五前发个更正。

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1497 -->
# 四级单词 1497 · 浸一浸；短暂游泳
中文：vt. 浸，蘸 vi. 浸一浸 n. 短暂游泳
单词：###dip###
搭配：###go for a dip### 去游个泳
例句：I'm sweating from the heat! Fancy a ###dip###? — Sure, but use caution; don't be impulsive and plunge straight in.
译文：我热得直冒汗！想去游一下吗？——好啊，不过要小心，别冲动地一下子跳进去。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1826 -->

单词：###caution###
搭配：###use caution### 小心行事
例句：I'm sweating from the heat! Fancy a dip? — Sure, but use ###caution###; don't be impulsive and plunge straight in.
译文：我热得直冒汗！想去游一下吗？——好啊，不过要小心，别冲动地一下子跳进去。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1827 -->

中文：adj. 冲动的
单词：###impulsive###
搭配：###an impulsive decision### 冲动的决定
例句：I'm sweating from the heat! Fancy a dip? — Sure, but use caution; don't be ###impulsive### and plunge straight in.
译文：我热得直冒汗！想去游一下吗？——好啊，不过要小心，别冲动地一下子跳进去。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1499 -->

搭配：###plunge into the water### 跳进水里
例句：I'm sweating from the heat! Fancy a dip? — Sure, but use caution; don't be impulsive and ###plunge### straight in.

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1500 -->
# 四级单词 1500 · 绳；电线
中文：n. 细绳，粗线，索；电线
单词：###cord###
搭配：###an electric cord### 电线
例句：Your ###cords### are such a mess! — I know. I'll coil them up, put them in a box, and keep the drawer orderly, like an electrician.
译文：你的电线乱成一团了！——我知道。我会像电工那样把它们绕好，放进盒子，让抽屉整整齐齐。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1501 -->

搭配：###coil up### 盘起来
例句：Your cords are such a mess! — I know. I'll ###coil### them up, put them in a box, and keep the drawer orderly, like an electrician.
译文：你的电线乱成一团了！——我知道。我会像电工那样把它们绕好，放进盒子，让抽屉整整齐齐。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1503 -->

单词：###orderly###
搭配：###an orderly room### 整洁的房间
例句：Your cords are such a mess! — I know. I'll coil them up, put them in a box, and keep the drawer ###orderly###, like an electrician.
译文：你的电线乱成一团了！——我知道。我会像电工那样把它们绕好，放进盒子，让抽屉整整齐齐。

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1828 -->

中文：n. 电器技师，电工，电气专家
单词：###electrician###
搭配：###call an electrician### 叫电工
例句：Your cords are such a mess! — I know. I'll coil them up, put them in a box, and keep the drawer orderly, like an ###electrician###.
译文：你的电线乱成一团了！——我知道。我会像电工那样把它们绕好，放进盒子，让抽屉整整齐齐。

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1504 -->
# 四级单词 1504 · 一口
中文：n. 满口，一口，少量
单词：###mouthful###
搭配：###a mouthful of food### 一口食物
例句：Try this blue cheese! — Just a ###mouthful###... Oh, may I spit it out? — Well, I'm inclined to agree; few find it favourable at first.
译文：尝尝这块蓝纹奶酪！——就一口……噢，我能吐出来吗？——嗯，我也有同感，很少有人第一次就喜欢。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1505 -->

搭配：###spit out### 吐出
例句：Try this blue cheese! — Just a mouthful... Oh, may I ###spit### it out? — Well, I'm inclined to agree; few find it favourable at first.
译文：尝尝这块蓝纹奶酪！——就一口……噢，我能吐出来吗？——嗯，我也有同感，很少有人第一次就喜欢。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1506 -->
